Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The topology operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 398. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 944 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_398(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 398. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^944(S, F) is trivial. QED.
